ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u10	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! I have the honour to report to the Health Commission on the draft law amending certain laws in order to ensure that during the period of notification of the epidemic or epidemic of medical personnel (print No 763). Marshal of the Sejm, according to Art. Article 37 (1) (a) and (b) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 1 and Article 1 Article 40 (1) (a) and (b) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 On 19 November 2020, 1 of the Rules of Procedure of the Sejm sent the above draft law to the Health Commission for first reading. The amendments proposed in the draft law amending certain laws in order to ensure that during the period of notification of an epidemic or epidemic of medical personnel, changes are primarily aimed at adapting health legislation to the fight against the epidemic of Coronavirus. These include, in particular, medical personnel: doctors, dentists, nurses, midwives, paramedics. This is intended to enable, on simplified terms, medical personnel from third countries, i.e. outside the European Union, to work in the Republic of Poland. In addition, the provisions of the project introduce regulations simplifying the rules for the return to the medical profession of persons who have a break in the pursuit of the profession for more than 5 years by replacing the obligation to engage in professional training under the supervision of another experienced professional. The draft law also provides for various types of regulation and adjustments resulting from the condition of the outbreak of Coronavirus. At the Health Committee meeting, which lasted quite long, substantive and editorial amendments were considered. The Health Commission, following its first reading and the examination of this draft law at its meeting on 26 November 2020, asks the High Sejm to approve the attached draft law. Thank you. [[Chairman of the proceedings includes Deputy Marshal Małgorzata Kidawa-Błońska Sejm]]
ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u11	Thank you very much, sir. The Sejm agreed that in the discussion on this item it would listen to the five-minute statements on behalf of the clubs and the circle. I'm opening a discussion. Mrs Barbara Dziuk, Parliamentary Club Law and Justice, will be the first to speak.
ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u12	Mrs. Marshal! Minister! High Izbo! On behalf of the Law and Justice Club, I present my position on the Law amending certain laws in order to ensure that during the period of notification of the epidemic or epidemic of medical personnel, print No 763. The amendments proposed in the draft law aim at adapting health legislation to the fight against the COVID-19 epidemic. The bill deals with medical personnel: doctors, dentists, nurses and midwives, as well as paramedics, and aims to enable medical personnel from outside the European Union to work in our country. It introduces two types of solutions: permanent solutions, which will also apply after the outbreak, and emergency solutions in place during the period of threat. It proposes to return to the medical profession for persons who have a break in their profession longer than 5 years. It also assumes that by 31 December 2021 the voivode may entrust the operation of a medical dispatcher to the medical emergency unit's dispatcher, while the team's head may be a medical emergency worker or nurse without the requirement of a 5-year professional experience. The Act also provides for a delay in the date of entry into force of the provisions concerning the start of certain activities by the National Monitoring Centre for Medical Rescue. It also provides for an amendment of the provisions on medical storage facilities for medical rescue teams. The Act also provides for the introduction of medical on-call options by healthcare professionals regardless of their university or secondary education. It also regulates the possibility of recording and storing conversations conducted in the system of State Medical Rescue, clarifies the composition of basic medical rescue teams, and provides for the extension of the data catalogue in the system of records of health resources. It is very important in the draft law that these rules are compatible with European Union law. The draft law does not require it to be submitted to the competent institutions and bodies of the European Union, including the European Central Bank, for the purpose of obtaining an opinion, notification, consultation or agreement. Law and Justice positively recommends the proposed amendments and also proposes five amendments. Thank you. [[Applause]]

When it comes to medical competitions, the return of doctors, nurses, laboratory diagnostics or pharmacists after a 5-year period of non-professional activity is a minor improvement. These people are not undergoing training now, i.e. during COVID, but they work in their workplace under the supervision of an experienced, qualified person, namely a doctor, nurse or pharmacist, respectively. Therefore, this period of 3 months is necessary for this person to prepare for this occupation. In general, the whole of Europe is in such a direction that most medical professions are multi-specialist. All countries are trying to use as much as possible, transfer their powers, move the rules just in case of the fight against the COVID. This is also the case in Poland. We've already changed some laws. The obstetrician on COVID time may carry out additional health benefits. The direction is that benefits may also be provided in the case of other professions. It goes on, among other things. about the vaccination, as was mentioned. We are talking mainly about vaccinations for COVID. As planned in January, there will be a great deal of opportunities for vaccines. Therefore, you have to be ready to inoculate a large number of people. Of course vaccination for COVID was such a question, they are, are to be free, recommended and optional vaccinations. That's why it's not... In the first place for patients in the most vulnerable groups, i.e. medical staff and the elderly. When it comes to influenza vaccinations, because this was also the case here, for the first time in history, medical personnel were very interested in vaccinations. Over 250,000 have been vaccinated. persons. The Ministry passed on the vaccines. The National Health Fund has paid many individuals to vaccinate its medical staff and others to whom it has not paid, because there were those who were not interested in payment, we simply passed the vaccine free of charge to these people. As far as this law is concerned, it was said that the year is coming to an end and there is no telling what is next with the salaries of nurses, doctors or paramedics, because most agreements are just ending on 31 December. Therefore, this law extends this period by another six months so that there is no situation that someone will not receive the funds that are due to him. This period is completely extended. At the same time, a statutory amendment has been prepared which guarantees every person who received an additional salary that after 1 July he will not be reduced by this remuneration, that the employer will not reduce this remuneration, which is presented here. [[♪ The bell ♪]] This law also extends the different deadlines in the case of medical rescue, so that the situation in this area is correct. There is also a way out for medical rescuers who can work in an equivalent working time, as the Member said, within the normal work code, the opt-out. If the paramedic agrees, he will also be able to work in a higher range than the work code. Thank you very much.

The new version of the World Anti-Doping Code, together with these International Standards, will apply from 1 January 2021. The changes are technical, adaptable and are not debatable in our opinion. ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u132	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! I am pleased to present a report on the work of the Sejm Economic Commission on the draft law amending the Act on the Contract for Construction or Services, the Law - Public Procurement Law and some other laws. The bill was the subject of a committee meeting yesterday. First of all, I would like to thank all Members for their active participation in the work and their thorough reading. The essence of this project is primarily to adapt the provisions relating to concession works contracts to the new Public Procurement Law, which is due to enter into force on 1 January 2021. This is one of the successive work to adapt the legal system affected by public procurement law, which is the result of consultations during the current period of vacathio legis. Let us remind that the new Public Procurement Law entered into force, was prepared on 11 September 2019 with this long period of vacathio legis just so that this legal order was adapted to the new law. In this law, including the submission above all, the proposed amendments take into account the requests and opinions made by representatives and participants of the entire public procurement market, both the contracting party and the contractors. The most important changes that have been designed, particularly in the field of concessions, are the adaptation, as I said, of the provisions of the Public Procurement Law, including, above all, the exclusion from the obligation to apply the Act, the establishment of rules for communication between contracting entities and economic operators and the grounds for excluding economic operators from the concession contract procedure. With regard to changes in the Public Procurement Law, as these are also elements of this submission, the provisions of the new and more flexible procedure for contracts below the EU thresholds are clarified. Basic mode. The provisions have been repealed, and this is also the result of a long consultation on the so-called. small orders, i.e. orders worth between 50 and 130 thousand. zł. The prohibition on contracts for more than 4 years shall also be abolished in respect of the award of contracts. The Public Procurement Law in this submission also complements regulations on legal remedies, e.g. issues relating to the taking of evidence in appeal proceedings. The third law, which this submission amends, is also the Public-Private Partnership Act, where a catalogue of provisions of the new law is defined, for which the provisions of the Public Procurement Law will not apply. In addition to the adjustment, what are the expected effects of this submission? This is above all an increase in the transparency and legibility of the public procurement system. This is also a clarification and complement to the regulation of the new Public Procurement Law. Finally, and this is the strong emphasis placed by the submitter, the government side, in the end the long-term development of infrastructure and services with the financial commitment of the private sector, which in fact is served by the famous Public Procurement Law, which was adopted last year. Among the requirements, the regulations that adapt the bill to the Public Procurement Law are also e.g. the definition of thematic areas such as the definition of the contracting authority, the exclusion of the application of the concession contract law or the determination of the threshold of applicable concession contract rules to contracts concluded. Interpreting doubts that may arise in connection with the application have also been removed in order to best reflect EU and EU procurement directives. I kindly ask the High Sejm to accept the project and hand it over for further work. Thank you very much.
ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u133	Thank you very much. The Sejm agreed that in the discussion on this item it would listen to the five-minute statements on behalf of the clubs and the circle. I'm opening a discussion. As the first on behalf of the Parliamentary Club, Mr Kamil Bortniczuk, Mr Law and Justice.
ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u134	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! I was honored to present the position of the Parliamentary Club Law and Justice regarding the government draft law amending the Act on a Contract for Construction or Services and some other laws. The adoption of legislative action on this issue is due to the need to adapt the provisions of the concession contract law to the wording of the new regulations of the Act - Public Procurement Law, which enters into force on 1 January 2021. The concession contract law is closely linked to the law governing the operation of public contracts both in substance, terminology and editorial terms. Many institutions and issues are regulated in the same way as in public contracts and failure to comply with the concession contract law with the amendments introduced by the Public Procurement Law could lead to legal uncertainty as a result of differences in interpretation concerning identical legal institutions governed by those laws. Therefore, it is necessary to adapt the concession contract law accordingly to the changes introduced in the public procurement system. Issues from the concession area need to be corrected both in the concession contract law and in the provisions implementing the law - Public procurement law. These include, in particular, the question of the threshold, the application of the law, the definitions contained in the law, the grounds for exclusion of economic operators and legal protection before a mixed procurement court. The draft law also makes detailed amendments to the law itself - Public procurement law, which in particular concerns a simplified procedure below the Union thresholds, the National Board of Appeal, legal protection measures and the control system for the operation of contracts. The draft also includes a revision of the provision on the value of salaries. The draft law also amends the Public-Private Partnership Act. The essence of this change is to define a catalogue of provisions of the new law - Public Procurement Law, which will not apply to public-private partnership agreements. On behalf of the Parliamentary Club, Law and Justice, I declare my support for this regulation. At the same time, I submit to the Marshal an amendment aimed at updating the references to the Act - Public Procurement Law contained in the Act on Special Solutions related to the prevention, prevention and eradication of COVID-19.

ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u218	Mr. Marshal! High Seym! Minister! On behalf of the Committee on Infrastructure, I have the honour to present a report on a government draft law amending the Public Roads Act and some other laws. The draft is included in print No 719 and the committee's report is in print No 767. Let me remind you that this project entered the Sejm on 28 October this year. and was directed to first reading. On 20 November, the 21st meeting of the High Sejm held a first reading of this submission and was addressed to the Committee on Infrastructure for consideration. The project concerns the possibility of traffic on public roads of vehicles with an authorised single axle load of up to 11,5 t without the need for authorisation. However, it should be noted that the project allows for limits on the permissible axle pressure to be imposed by the road manager or by the local authority, and does not apply to roads with a ground surface on which the law only allows the movement of vehicles with a single axle pressure of up to 8 t. This solution is the implementation of the March 2019 judgment of the Court of Justice of the European Union. In that judgment The Court held that by requiring transport companies to have special permits to travel on certain public roads, the Republic of Poland failed to fulfil its obligations under the provisions of Article. 3 and 7 of the Council Directive of July 1996 establishing for certain road vehicles moving within the Community the maximum permissible dimensions in national and international traffic and the maximum permissible loads in international traffic. Therefore, it is necessary to make statutory amendments in order to remove or modify illegal national legislation in the Union and, unfortunately, it cannot be implemented in any other way. The draft law will result in the full implementation of the judgment of the TSUE and in ensuring compliance of national legislation with the provisions of the above-mentioned law. a directive, which means ensuring the mobility of vehicles with a permissible pressure of a single driving axle up to 11.5 t on public roads in Poland without the need to obtain authorisation to pass a non-normative vehicle. The Infrastructure Committee at its meeting yesterday considered this project. In the course of the committee's work on the draft, legislative comments and one substantive amendment were tabled. Following a vote, the Commission unanimously adopted the draft together with the amendment and proposes that it be adopted by the High Sejm. Thank you, Marshal. [[Applause]] Bravo!

ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u220	Mr. Marshal! High Seym! The draft law on amending the Public Roads Act and some other laws considered by the High House raised a discussion when working in the Infrastructure Commission after first reading, but at the same time did not cause much controversy as verified by the vote. This is the implementation of EU law, specifically the judgments of the Court of Justice of the European Union. To date, local authorities, either by independent decisions of local government executives or by the regulations of the road management agencies, have issued decisions allowing traffic on individual local roads mainly in the form of internal regulations which either limited the tonnage of vehicles or pressure of their single axle on the road. At the same time, exemptions were often created for means of transport for companies which were mentioned on information marks placed under signs restricting the movement of vehicles of a certain tonnage. This caused numerous interventions of transport companies, because vehicles of some companies, usually located near a particular section of the road, were allowed to operate on such a local road, and often vehicles of other companies, located a little further away, e.g. In another municipality or in another district, this movement was not allowed, so they had to look for other circular routes to their companies. I myself have experienced a number of civic interventions in this area, both in self-government work and in the exercise of a parliamentary mandate. On the basis of these experiences, I can say one thing: so far there have been no national rules that would regulate this area of socio-economic reality. Following the judgment of the TSUE of 19 March 2019, we have such a legal reality that all internal regulations on tonnage limitation or axle pressure are formally invalid, which few people know and what they are not really enforcing. Today, the above-mentioned provisions need to be regulated. cases on a national scale, allowing for restrictions, as referred to in Article. 1 amendment 2nd Act of Procededed Law. Numerous changes are made primarily in Article. 41 and the following Article shall be inserted: 41a, Art. 41b, Art. 41c and Art. 41d to the Public Road Act. If we lean on these changes, we will come to the conclusion that they regulate issues on a national scale, giving local governments, as road managers, the possibility of numerous exemptions and prohibitions which will be introduced either by means of regulations or, above all, by resolutions of a given municipality council or district. The most important of the proposed solutions are the provisions in Article. Article 41 (1) (a) and (b) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 4 and 5 Public Roads Act, which describes the conditions for the admission on local public roads of vehicles with high loads of a single axle on the road. The Law and Justice Club will support the amendments proposed by the Council of Ministers included in the procedated law implementing EU law. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u222	Thank you very much. Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! I am pleased to present the position of the Parliamentary Club Civic Coalition regarding the draft law amending the Public Roads Act and some other laws contained in print No 719. The need for changes in this respect arises from the judgment of the Court of Justice of the European Union of March 2019 in which the requirement for transport undertakings to hold special authorisations for the use of certain public roads was challenged. Among other things, it was decided that by requiring transport companies to have special permits to travel on certain public roads, the Republic of Poland violated Community rules by establishing for certain road vehicles moving within the Community the maximum permissible dimensions in national and international traffic and the maximum permissible burdens in international traffic. These are vehicles with a single driving axis of up to 11.5 t. The Court of Justice found the Polish system of authorising the carriage of such vehicles to be contrary to the provisions of the Council Directive. It is worth reminding that the national roads are 19 403 km, and local government roads are 405 160 km. At present, the free transport of vehicles with a 11.5 t pressure on the axle is possible in principle only on national roads and covers a total of 3% All roads. After the shift, it'll be 70%. The change will therefore largely affect local roads. We have analysed the provisions contained in the text of the Act. The introduction or establishment of a ban on the movement of vehicles with a 11,5 t axle shall be permitted on public roads or sections thereof whose technical characteristics or technical condition of the road surface structure are not adapted to the movement of vehicles with a single axle pressure of more than 10 t or more than 8 t and which at the same time meet inter alia. the following conditions: the road or section of the road passes through an area where there is a form of nature protection, exit from a nursery or a children's club, a kindergarten, a school or a care-care centre, in a historic area, where the road or part of the road passes through an intensive residential area, or the road does not have a sidewalk, a collisionless pedestrian crossing or a pedestrian crossing equipped with light signalling. It's a step in the right direction. We also appreciate that the Self-Government Road Fund Act introduces a criterion for raising technical standards for district and communal roads, in particular adapting them to the traffic of vehicles with a single driving axis of 11.5 t, and maintaining the uniformity of district roads and communal roads with regard to compliance with these standards. This should result in a significant increase in the pool of funds that should reach local authorities for the modernisation of existing infrastructure. For it is the municipalities and districts with the introduction of new regulations that will have the biggest problem, and it is they who will bear the greatest cost. We call, by way of support to the voice of local authorities, for the decision to grant the funding to be announced earlier and the funds from the Self-Government Road Fund to be shared according to clear, clear, fair and objective criteria, because this has so far been different. We believe that insufficient analysis has been made of the effects of the proposed solutions. Their impact on rail and inland waterway transport has not been analysed. We do not even know the approximate amount that will have to be spent on making changes. There has been no analysis of how these changes will affect the issue of ecology, air cleanliness, increased noise. The 60-day time to complete them in an era of pandemics and staff shortages may not be enough for local governments. It should be stressed, as has already been said, that the adoption of a law in this form will fundamentally change the perception of public roads in Poland. The implementation of the judgment of the Court of Justice of the European Union requires a fundamental discussion in Poland on the standards of reconstruction of roads and how they are financed and financed by government funds, [[Applause]] as well as creating the conditions for the development of alternative forms of communication - primarily railway. High Izbo! Members of the Civic Coalition are always responsible for the proposed laws. As I have already mentioned, it is primarily intended to bring Polish legislation into line with EU rules and for these reasons - despite the many doubts I have expressed - we will vote in favour of it. ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u223	Thank you very much, sir. I invite Mr Maciej Kopc, parliamentary club of the Left.
ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u224	Thank you. Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! The Government's draft law amending the Law on public roads and certain other laws, contained in print No 719, is the implementation of the judgment of the Court of Justice of the European Union of 21 March 2019, which ensures compliance of national legislation with Union law. The project concerns the ability to operate vehicles with a single axle pressure of up to 11,5 t without the need to obtain additional permits, but which can be limited by the road manager or local authorities. The only exception will be the ground roads. On these roads it will be possible to operate vehicles with a single axle pressure of up to 8 t, as these roads do not meet the relevant load requirements. This criterion applies to 29% of all public roads. Not all public roads meet technical standards that allow heavy vehicles to move on them, are threatened with rapid destruction and frequent renovations, so traffic bans with a pressure of up to 11.5 t can be introduced by the road manager or by a decision taken by the municipality or city council. Roads with a surface other than bituminous, concrete, stone or clinker shall be made by the standards recorded in the design analysed here with a single axle pressure of 8 t. Self-governments will be able to introduce restrictions on the movement of heavy vehicles up to 11,5 t when two legally defined conditions are met: when the technical condition of the road prevents heavy vehicles from moving on it and when the road runs near areas or objects to which heavy vehicles may have a particularly negative impact, e.g. runs or borders the protected area or is situated in the vicinity of residential buildings, kindergartens, schools, spas or mining areas. Traffic bans shall not be allowed on public roads or sections thereof, on the trans-European road network, on national roads or sections thereof, with the exception of national roads managed by city presidents on the rights of the county, on public roads or sections thereof which have been built or rebuilt with funds from the budget of the European Union. The prohibition shall not be applicable for 5 years from the date of entry into service of the road or section thereof. As part of raising technical standards for district and municipal roads, an additional criterion was introduced in the application for funding from the Self-Government Road Fund: adjusting the road to the traffic of vehicles with permissible axle pressure to 11,5 t. This means the possibility for local authorities to receive additional financial support, which will adapt local roads to the traffic of heavy vehicles. Importantly, for transport operators, provision is made for provisions which will result in the recovery of fees paid by the TSUE prior to the judgment and the remission of authorisation proceedings or penalties for the absence of such fees. Carriers who have paid the fee and paid the administrative penalty for the lack of authorisation before the judgment is delivered by the TSUE for exceeding the single-drive pressure axis to 11,5 t will have a month to submit an appropriate application, counting from the date of entry into force of the amended law, and thus a chance to recover the money. There will be no need to obtain authorisations for the passage of a single axle up to 11,5 t, which means simplifying administrative procedures and reducing the number of documents issued. As a result, it will be easier and cheaper to carry out transport activities. It will be possible to travel heavy vehicles without authorisation, e.g. to logistics and transhipment terminals, which can accelerate the development of an economic activity requiring such transport. The Left Club will vote in favour of the Government's draft Print Act No 719. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u296	Thank you very much, Marshal. High Izbo! I have the honour to present on behalf of the Parliamentary Club Law and Justice a position towards the Government's draft law amending the Law on Special Solutions related to the prevention, prevention and eradication of COVID-19, other infectious diseases and the crisis situations caused by them. The basis for the amendment of the Act is the creation of a legal basis allowing a worker or another person employed in isolation at home due to a positive test for SARS-CoV-2 virus to perform remote work. The legislator has regulated this issue in the case of quarantine. The Act proposes the introduction of an equivalent solution enabling domestic isolation workers who do not exhibit symptoms of COVID-19 disease or whose symptoms are mild in nature, to perform remote work. In the event of a deterioration in the worker's health during his/her remote work during his/her home isolation, he/she shall be entitled to sickness pay. In order to harmonise the proposed solutions, the issue of benefits granted during the period of incapacity for work during the quarantine period as well as during the period of isolation in the home environment has been regulated. The Act also proposes to allow suspended medical, psychological, training and testing of train drivers' knowledge and periodic examinations. The proposed mechanism will make it easier for rail medicine to carry out the necessary research both during and after the outbreak. In addition, what the proposed law amends is that the entry into force of the above amendments is equivalent to the restoration of the legal status in force prior to the entry into force of the Law of 31 March 2020 amending the Law on Special Solutions related to the prevention and prevention and eradication of COVID-19 and other communicable diseases and the crisis situations caused by them and certain other laws concerning the appointment of members of the Council of Social Dialogue. The proposed amendment also introduces an occasional solution to abolish the trade ban on Sunday 6 December 2020 in commercial establishments. In December 2020, two commercial Sundays for 13 and 20 December were provided for in the Act. The pre-Christmas period, especially before Christmas, is a period in which the number of customers purchasing is significant. In order to decompose this process over time and thus reduce the number of customers shopping at the same time, it is appropriate to introduce an additional commercial Sunday on 6 December. On the one hand, this will allow the maintenance of sanitary restrictions due to the ongoing epidemic and on the other hand will have a positive impact on entrepreneurs, contributing to improving their situation. Designed and proposed solutions are the result of requests made by both employees and employers. In view of the above, Parliamentary Club Law and Justice asks for a positive opinion on the above draft law. Thank you. [[Applause]]
ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u297	Thank you very much. I invite Mrs Iwona Maria Kozłowska from the Parliamentary Club Civic Coalition. Good morning.
ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u298	Good morning. Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Minister! I have the honour to present the position of the Civic Coalition Parliamentary Club on draft laws amending the Law on Special Solutions related to the prevention, prevention and eradication of COVID-19, other communicable diseases and the crisis situations caused by them and certain other laws, prints No 772, 773 and 778. An important change in the draft law in print No 772 is the regulation of remote work during the illness caused by SARS-CoV-19 during the quarantine period and during the isolation period. The regulation will allow many people to retain their right to pay. It should be borne in mind that one of the basic obligations of the employer is to provide workers with safe and hygienic working conditions. During the pandemic, this obligation becomes particularly important. Moreover, by targeting the remote work of infected workers, the employer protects other employees. About 16.5 million people are active in Poland. According to Rzeczpospolita, during the second wave of pandemics, about 1/3 of the workers could switch to remote work, i.e. more than 5 million people. For people who have a positive SARS-CoV-2 virus test and have mild and asymptomatic disease, remote work with 100% payout is a better option than 80% sick pay salary. The remuneration for the duration of the disease shall be payable for a period of not more than 33 days of the disease. This remuneration shall be paid by the employer. If the insured person exceeds the limit of 33 days, he/she shall be entitled to a sickness benefit which is paid directly by the Social Insurance Institution. Thus, for quarantine or isolation periods, it may generally be paid for the duration of the illness or sickness benefit. Parliamentary Club Civic Coalition proposes to pay 100% the basis for the size of the allowance during the quarantine period, isolation. We propose that the benefit be paid from the Social Insurance Fund and that it actually amounts to 100%. The draft law also included a provision restoring the legal status in force prior to the entry into force of the Law of 31 March 2020 concerning the dismissal of members of the Council of Social Dialogue. The special act adopted at the end of March allowed the Prime Minister to dismiss members of the board appointed by employers or trade unions during the period of the epidemic threat. Among other things, he protested against these solutions. President of the National Commission of the NSZZ.Solidarity, which pointed out that the back door was introduced into the Act, which undermines the sovereignty and limits the independence of the social partners in the Council of Social Dialogue by granting the President of the Council of Ministers the power to dismiss the members of the Council, unfortunately not only for illustrative reasons but also for other, more indeterminate reasons. After the changes, as is apparent from the draft law, the Prime Minister will no longer have the power to dismiss the members of the Social Dialogue Council because, as stated, there is no possibility of a transparent, substantive and regular dialogue. The proposed amendment of the Act of 10 January 2018 on the restriction of trade on Sundays and holidays and on certain other days introduces an episodeary solution to abolish trade on Sunday 6 December of this year. This is a legitimate change as the pre-Christmas period is a particular time of intensive shopping and in order to spread this process over time, thus reducing the number of customers shopping, it is justified to introduce an additional commercial Sunday. It's a shame you're doing this so late. It's almost 60%. Poles want a complete temporary abolition of the trade ban on Sundays during the pandemic, and only 27% It's against the idea. And here's a little curiosity, ladies and gentlemen. Even the voters of Law and Justice would like to abolish the trade ban on Sundays. It's 53.5%. those who support the ruling party. Therefore, the Civic Coalition proposes to return to commercial Sundays in 2021. In the current situation, despite the recommendations related to social distance, queues are still being created in shops, mainly on Saturdays. This is particularly important for older people, disabled people, carers, for whom safety rules for wearing masks, disinfection and social distance are important, so that without contact with a large number of people, minimise the risk of getting sick or getting infected by a person under their care. [[♪ The bell ♪]] The second point concerns the economic and economic situation. There's about 400,000 in shopping malls. persons. If brands that can't make up for their losses start shutting down unprofitable stores, thousands of jobs will disappear, which today are mainly filled by young people, students. Etats can also disappear from companies operating shopping malls. Each shopping gallery has a dozen contracts with external companies signed today. As economists point out, Poland is entering the recession phase with very low GDP. Trade is a very important pillar of economic development in this GDP. Therefore, the Civic Coalition Parliamentary Club amends its amendment to extend the abolition of trade on Sundays for the whole 2021 year. The Civic Coalition Parliamentary Club will vote in favour of this project, but we are making three amendments. In addition to the amendment, which concerns 100% the basis for the benefit to be paid from the Social Insurance Fund for the period of incapacity for work due to SARS-CoV-2 virus disease, we propose abolishing the trade ban on Sunday 2021 and propose that winter holidays in the school year 2020/2021 last between 4 January 2021 and 14 March 2021. Thank you very much. Mr. Marshal, I'm making appropriate adjustments. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u334	Thank you very much. Mr. Marshal! Minister! High Izbo! I am pleased to present the position of the Parliamentary Club Civic Coalition towards the government's draft law amending the Act on Taxation on Goods and Services and the Act - Bank law upon presentation of the report of committees, prints Nos 718 and 759. Of course, every time someone puts forward a law, a draft regulation that refers to certain facilitations, simplifications or phrases that make life easier for entrepreneurs, it should be welcomed, but on the other hand it should also be carefully analysed, because many times in history, such projects, which were presented as great facilitations, to meet entrepreneurs, turned out to be after careful analysis of the consequences, but not so great. And it's a little similar with this project. Not all the proposals contained in the government's bill meet, eliminate bureaucracy. I have the impression that there will be much more on some of the proposals for this bureaucracy. That is why we have allowed ourselves to formulate and present to the High House in second reading the nine amendments that relate to the substance of the law. These amendments show the direction in which the government should go if it really wants to make business easier if it wants entrepreneurs to do what they should do, namely business, rather than bureaucratic issues and the fulfilment of various official duties. We propose that, in the case of low value goods which are transferred by taxpayers, two burdensome records should be removed and, instead, an obligation and a restriction on the value of these goods should be introduced. We propose that it be 50 PLN. It's about removing two records and creating one for this place. That's how easy it is. Such facilitations today are expected and this is the amendment we are making. When it comes to lips. 13, which the Ministry so praised as to remove the obligation to have proof of the corrective invoice, was unfortunately converted by the Ministry to have a more indeterminate documentation which the taxpayer should produce under certain commercial arrangements. It turns out that even the Legislative Office had very serious doubts about what this project was doing during the process. This provision, which has already been granted by the Ministry itself, will require some more detailed discussion, the issue of guidelines. Therefore, we believe that it is absolutely unacceptable that such legislation as it will constitute, I apologise for the expression, the hammer on the entrepreneurs who will be used by the officials, be adopted by the High House. We therefore propose that we supplement our mouths. 13 in such a way as to add correspondence to this definition. This is a simple way to agree on business terms today. e-mail correspondence, e-mail exchange - these are today's tools that entrepreneurs use. This will completely exhaust the problem and, on the other hand, protect the state's budget against losses. We are also eliminating the wording for established trading conditions. This solution in practice introduces an important administrative barrier to the efficient, safe use of tax free. Contrary to the assertions set out in the explanatory memorandum of the bill, the requirement to obtain a clear travel signature does not function under the current rules. The introduction of a condition of readability is an additional barrier for entrepreneurs operating in this system. It should also be stressed that there is no definition of the legal concept of a legible signature and that the interpretation of this phrase is not clear. Another issue raised by the business community is the obligation to return in Polish currency in non-cash transactions. They draw attention to the practical dimension of the obligation to refund in Polish currency in the case of a non-cash refund and to the contradiction of this obligation with the assumption of promotion. This regulation often results in the traveler being burdened in practice at the expense of additional conversions several times in the event of a refund on the payment card. In the functioning of the currency settlement model on the market in card systems, the traveller receiving a refund in Polish zlotys on a payment card often incurs multiple costs of conversion. First the euro per dollar and then the cost of conversion into the currency in which the card account is held. This increases the costs of handling transactions up to 10% the amount of the cash refund, and therefore we believe that it is appropriate to request that the wording of the article be amended accordingly. Article 127 (1) (b) and (c) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 5 of the VAT Act limit the obligation to apply the Polish currency only to cash returns. However, these comments do not alter the overall assessment. Our task is to continue working on this bill, in order to meet the expectations of Polish entrepreneurs. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u351	Dear Marshal, High Izbo! In the first words, I would like to thank Members for their very warm reception of this project. This also shifts the moment when I can make this correction. This is particularly tedious, given the fact that between entrepreneurs very often this turnover is very intense, there may be even thousands of invoices per month. In case of some mistake, the need for correction is also a long waiting time and just another formality that needs to be implemented. Now the proposal is that, like me as an entrepreneur, I will once make an agreement with my contractor on how to correct it so that it always works. We're not talking about any more vague documentation, we're talking about some freedom, flexibility. It is not for us in any way to punish entrepreneurs or to look for reasons why an invoice should not be taken into account, but to leave them the widest possible catalogue of possibilities to carry out the correction. And as it is in a flexible way written in the text of the Act, so in the explanations that will be issued to it, it will still be confirmed with protective power for the taxpayer and binding for the administration that it is the taxpayer who decides how he will date his counterparty, so that it will simply be understandable and safe for both parties. As regards the method of fixing the exchange rates, it is a request that has been made by the National Tax Administration when simplifying, or the possibility of changing the way in which currency differences are determined from the VAT method to the one characteristic of income taxes, it is important that the payers also do not attempt to manipulate exchange rates, e.g. by repeatedly changing this method over a year, which is time-consuming on the one hand, and on the other hand is difficult to observe by the administration. Also as a result, through the benefits generated in this way, such entrepreneurs will become more competitive towards their competitors, those who focus on their activities rather than on changes in the way they settle exchange rate differences. ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u357	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Minister! On behalf of the Parliamentary Club, Law and Justice, I present my position on the government draft law amending the Act on the Christian Theological Academy in Warsaw, prints no. 678 and 756. The aim of the proposed amendment is to modify the funding rules of the university guaranteeing its stable activities. The Christian Theological Academy in Warsaw is the only university in the country that educates theological staff for the needs of minority Christian churches operating in Poland. The Academy conducts studies in the following fields: theology, pedagogy, social work, and since last year in the academic field: pre-school and early-school pedagogy. In the academic year 2018, the Academy studied 343 students and as of 31 December 2018, a total of 37.2 academic staff were employed at the Academy. In 2020, the amount of grants to the academy for the maintenance and development of teaching potential and for the maintenance and development of research capacity amounted to 8297 thousand. PLN and the amount of the subjective grant amounted to 750 thousand. zł. The Christian Theological Academy in Warsaw, through its unique educational activity, has fewer opportunities compared to other universities in terms of obtaining good parameters included in the allocation algorithms for public universities. It should therefore be concluded that in order for the Christian Theological Academy to function properly, it is necessary to introduce detailed solutions related to its financing. The aim is to introduce a provision guaranteeing the academy to receive subsidies from funding for higher education and education of not less than the amount fixed for the previous year. Thus, if, as a result of the evaluation of the quality of scientific activities on the basis of which an algorithm is calculated different for academic institutions and another for professional universities, the subsidy would be lower than in the previous year, it will automatically be increased by means of State funds, from Part 28: Higher education and science, at the disposal of the Minister responsible for Higher Education and Science. The Act also proposes to maintain the subjective grant granted to the Academy by the Minister responsible for religious faiths and national and ethnic minorities while defining the scope of this grant. The Minister will provide grants to finance the remuneration of academic teachers working in the theology department together with income from these salaries. The Act proposes to adapt the nomenclature in accordance with the applicable Higher Education Act. Parliamentary Club Law and Justice, taking the above into account, will vote in favour of this law. Thank you very much.

ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u376	Thank you very much. I'm opening a discussion. I would like to present the position of the Parliamentary Club, the Law and Justice of Mr Tomasz Zieliński.
ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u377	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the Parliamentary Club, Law and Justice, I present my position on the government draft law amending the name of the University of Arts in Poznań, prints no. 645 and 681. The presented bill assumes a change in the name of the University of Arts in Poznań to the University of Arts. Magdalena Abakanowicz in Poznań. Magdalena Abakanowicz was born on June 20, 1930 in Fallenty near Raszyn. After graduating from the Academy of Fine Arts in Warsaw, she began her great art adventure with painting. Between 1954 and 1959, her work consisted of large canvas sheets painted with watercolors. In 1960, the first individual exhibition of the artist took place in the Cordegard Gallery in Warsaw, and in 1965 she was awarded a gold medal at the biennial in Sao Paulo. In the '60s. Magdalena Abakanowicz began making unusual sculptures for which she used fabrics, giving them three-dimensionality, hanging at the ceiling. These works, which have been called abacans since her name, have brought her international fame and artistic independence. In the '70s. Magdalena Abakanowicz has established her international position with the cycle "Alterations" including the series "Heads," "Back," "Sitting" and "Embriology," which was enthusiastically accepted at the Venice Biennale in 1980. The '80s. The 20th century is a series of works performed in traditional sculpting materials - stone, wood and bronze. During that time, one of the most original exhibitions of the artist was created: War Games, during which she presented wooden logs wrapped in fabrics or atonement with steel rims, which were supposed to resemble war machines. In the same years Magdalena Abakanowicz is carrying out great projects in the open air, among others. in Israel, Germany, the United States and, of course, Poland, where one of the largest projects is located: "Unrecognized" - 112 over 2 metres of cast iron figures in the Poznań Citadel Park performed by Abakanowicz on the occasion of the 750th anniversary of Poznań. The artist presented her works at more than 200 exhibitions on all continents, winning a number of awards and distinctions. She won the competition announced by the Paris authorities. She created the concept of arboreal architecture, i.e. 25-metre tree houses. Magdalena Abakanowicz has been awarded numerous times by the authorities in Poland and abroad. Eight times the title of Dr. Honoris causa has been given. She was an honorary member of the four art academy. Magdalena Abakanowicz was associated with today's University of Arts in Poznań in the years 1965 and 1990, where she has been conducting classes since 1979 as a professor with students. She was also a guest lecturer at universities in the United States, Australia, and Japan. [[♪ The bell ♪]] Magdalena Abakanowicz has been awarded numerous times in Poland and abroad, including the Commander's Cross with the Star of the Order of Rebirth of Poland. Parliamentary Club Law and Justice, expressing appreciation for the achievements of the outstanding Polish artist and taking into account the relationships of Magdalena Abakanowicz with the Poznań university, will vote in favour of the adoption of this Act. Thank you. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u407	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Minister! On behalf of the Parliamentary Club, Law and Justice, I present my position on the government draft law on changing the name of the Academy of Music in Cracow, prints no. 722 and 757. The presented bill assumes a change in the name of the university: Academy of Music in Cracow to: Academy of Music in Cracow. Krzysztof Penderecki in Kraków. Krzysztof Penderecki, born on 23 November 1933 in Dębica, died on 29 March 2020 in Cracow, was one of the largest Polish composers, he was an outstanding conductor and educator. Krzysztof Penderecki has been associated with the Academy of Music in Krakow all his adult life. The beginnings of this Krakow university date back to 1888, when the Conservatory of Music was established, which after the end of World War II resumed its activity as the State Conservatory of Krakow. In 1946 the State Higher School of Music was established, which in 1979 was named: Academy of Music in Cracow. Krzysztof Penderecki studied composition at the State Higher School of Music in 1955. After finishing his studies, he started working as a composer at the university. Between 1972 and 1987 he served as the rector. For the rest of his life, he worked with the Academy of Music, ran the orchestra of the university on several occasions, gave patronage to the most important projects - international competitions, supported the Academy in many organisational and artistic matters. The idea of giving the Academy of Music the name of Krzysztof Penderecki was born in the Chair of Composition and finally formulated by the Senate of the Academy by a resolution of 22 June 2020. This application was accepted by the composer's wife Elizabeth Penderecka and other members of his family. Krzysztof Penderecki's great achievements include operas, symphonies, orchestral, chamber, instrumental or choir frames of religious texts. He created a new direction in contemporary music called sonorism. Krzysztof Penderecki's compositions have been repeatedly used by renowned film directors. Among the many works of Krzysztof Penderecki are Tren - Victims of Hiroshima, Passion according to St. Łukasz, symphony or Polish Requiem. Krzysztof Penderecki received many significant and prestigious awards for his work around the world, including among others. Four Grammy Awards. He received the title of doctor honoris causa at 30 universities. He was awarded many times, including the Order of the White Eagle - the highest Polish State Award. The Parliamentary Club Law and Justice, expressing its appreciation for the achievements of an outstanding artist and taking into account the relationship between Krzysztof Penderecki and the Krakow University, will vote in favour of the adoption of this Act. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

Penderecki's style has evolved for decades, but his music has never ceased to be special. Penderecki created electronic music in the 1960s. In the 20th century, he studied her potential, returned to classical music and achieved the same with the orchestra. He used traditional instruments in a non-standard way and was always original. A great teacher, a man of great heart, a phenomenal musician who did not divide music into classical and popular, but into good and bad. ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u423	Dear Marshal, High Izbo! On behalf of the Parliamentary Club, I present my position on the draft resolution tabled by the Bureau of the Sejm on the establishment of 2021 by the Year of Constitution 3 May, prints No 381 and 629. On 3 May 1791, the Grand Sejm, chaired by Marshal Stanisław Małachowski, adopted the first in Europe and the second in the world modern basic law. The Constitution of May 3 was the result of the work of the most outstanding representatives of Polish enlightenment, and its main task was to repair the ossified system of the Republic of Poland of both Nards based on the so-called. golden noble freedom preventing the proper functioning of the state. At that time, members of the patriotic biases led by Ignacy Potocki, supported by King Stanisław August Poniatowski, knew that reforms were necessary to strengthen the executive power and to create a strong army guaranteeing independence from Russia and promising to regain the land lost during the first cut. The Constitution's provisions on 3 May introduced the Montesque threefold division of power into the legislative power: the two-room parliament, the executive: the king and the guardian of laws, and the judiciary. Free selection was eliminated, introducing heredity of the throne. Liberum veto has been removed. The poor nobleman's influence on state policy has been limited, the laws of the townsfolk have been increased and the first step has been taken towards protection by the country of peasants. On 20 October 1791, the Sejm adopted the Mutual Engagement of the two Nations, which was an implementing act for the Constitution and included closer links between Poland and Lithuania going beyond the Lublin Union. Acting only 14 months, the Constitution on 3 May was permanently registered in the history of Poland and Lithuania as an attempt to repair the state. It is associated with the pursuit of the enlightened layers of society to gain independence from the influence of foreign powers. Over the years of the Constitutional partitions on 3 May, the Polish-Russian war in defence of the Constitution and the Kościuszko insure were events for many Poles who determined their attitude against the assassins. May 12th this year The Sejm of the Republic of Lithuania unanimously established the year 2021 as the Year of Constitution 3 May and the Engagement of the Mutual Both Nations, asking the Polish Sejm to adopt a similar resolution. Therefore, taking into account the 230th anniversary of the adoption of the Constitution in 2021, the Parliamentary Club Law and Justice supports the adoption by the Sejm of a resolution establishing the year 2021 by the Year of Constitution 3 May. Thank you very much.

When pressure became too violent, memorable, famous words came out: Non possumus. This Primate of the Millennium was the initiator of the Great Novena millennium on the occasion of the millennium of Poland's baptism, with symbolic peregrination of the painting of Madonna Jasna Góra after all parishes, he arranged an act of renewal of Jasna Góra vows and he was the advocate of the letter of Polish bishops to German bishops from 1965. In 1950 Primate Wyszyński as head of the Polish Church signed an agreement with the authorities of the Polish People's Republic. Three years later, the government broke the deal, and the primate was arrested. It was then that he said his famous: Non possumus. The place of his imprisonment was Ryvald, Stokzek, Prudnik and Komańcza in Bieszczady. An important step on the way of Polish-German relations was inspired by the Primate's message of Polish bishops addressed to German bishops with a historical opinion: We forgive and ask forgiveness. As part of the retortion, the authorities of the Polish People's Republic organized a chase against bishops and the Primate, but it was the cardinal's story that was right, because without the message of bishops it would not be easy to get Polish-German closer and a gesture of reconciliation. When a massacre occurred on the coast in 1970, the cardinal took the side of the workers. Between 1980 and 1981, he brokered negotiations between the authorities and the emerging "Solidarity." He was a moral authority, a co-creator of social changes that led to the collapse of communism. For many today's hierarchs, it remains an unsurpassed pattern. In June 1983 in Warsaw about the Primate of the Millennium, the Pope said: He was a free man, he taught us, his compatriots, true freedom. He was a tireless spokesperson of the dignity of every man and good name of Poland (the bell) among the peoples of Europe and the world. He was born in Lviv in a Jewish family, but at the same time went to religious classes, and he spoke about himself as an unbeliever. Complicated personality. As a young man, he was tested during an intelligence study and described as the most intelligent student of the whole part of Poland. So unusual intelligence, insight of the mind, but also interesting family. The family had two tenements. When the communists took over the area, he was declared a family of landowners. For this reason, when the repatriation took place, he moved with his family to Poland, to Krakow, losing the entire estate. During the German occupation he hid under a false name, fearing that he would be taken into captivity, because then all the Jews were destroyed, and they were sent to the ghetto. But after the war... During the war, he participated in the Resistance, hid one of his friends of Jewish origin, cooperated with the Resistance, so it can be said that he also had a beautiful patriotic card. After the war he moved to Poland and here he also met - even despite his own, one can say, views and in early youth looking into the communist system - he collided with this system. Yes, he had great problems because he once criticized communism in his writings, criticized Stalinism, baldness, and therefore had difficulties in his life. But the most important thing for which we worship this character is its wonderful work. Creativity, which, you can say, has set new directions not only in science fiction, but also in criticism. He wrote scripts, columnies, essays, literary journalism. Although, ladies and gentlemen, he is most famous for writing great science fiction novels. His rich work was even the reason that the American Academy considered the possibility that a team of writers was established in Poland, who under the pseudonym Lem - because some even suspected that it was an alias - write all kinds of such comprehensive literature. Later, of course, it was explained and Lem was considered one of the most outstanding writers. He was even a candidate for the Nobel Prize. He didn't get it, but his work is actually outstanding. She has made a great contribution to culture not only Polish but also worldly. The Commission recommends that the Sejm adopt a resolution to commemorate [[♪ The bell ♪]] This great writer next year. ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u489	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Stanisław Lem was an outstanding writer, philosopher of Earth and space, lion, Pole, Jew. 30 million copies of his books were released. It has been translated into over 40 languages. He had so many, he didn't open the translation packages. These books were gathering in his house, there were piles of them. I was honored to meet him in person. Stanisław Lem knew my father for half a century, and for the last 24 years of his life he was very close to him, they were in great friendship. My father later said he had no personal secrets from Lem or political secrets. Given that his father was a natural conspirator, it was very unusual that he became so close to Lem at the time of martial law. My father even had a key to the Lem family's home in Vienna and sometimes lived there for weeks while they were elsewhere. This enabled me to meet Lem not only as the author of fascinating books that I read as a teenager, but also as a private person. Stanisław Lem was an introverted, power-enthusiast man, focused on writing, which is by itself a solitary activity, on his wife Barbara and on his son Thomas. He was the son of the famous Lviv laryngologist Samuel Lem, an officer of the Austrian Tsar army, whose miraculously saved one of his Jewish patients from being shot by the Bolsheviks. Because this particular patient served in the Russian army. Thanks to his father... That few people know, Stanisław Lem was also Marian Hemar's aunt's brother. That probably doesn't tell you much because you're young. But Marian Hemar, or rather Hescheles, was an outstanding and well-known satirist, author of thousands of songs, soldier of the independent Carpathian Rifle Brigade near Tobruk, and employee of the Polish Radio Free Europe after the war. Stanisław Lem had a very happy childhood - he grew up as an only child in a wealthy and well-known Lviv family. His youth, however, looked completely different. In May 1939 he graduated, and already in September his beloved city of Lviv was occupied by the Soviets. Lem, considered by the Soviets to be a bourgeois, could not study at the Lviv University of Technology, but managed to get into the medical department of the university. In June 1941, after the German invasion, the Lemów family was imprisoned in a transitional camp on the Lviv Sand. Thanks to the help of former high school colleagues who were members of the OGM, he organized an escape and hid his parents from his former housekeeper. The ZWZ colleagues made them great papers, which allowed Lem to work as a locksmith in the Wehrmacht car workshops. There, trained by the AGM, repaired the engines of these cars (the bell) that were going... Mr. Marshal, let me... ...to the east front, that after a few hundred kilometers they got off and were unusable. The same Lem, thanks to these hard papers - because such papers that you work for the Wehrmacht, it was a miracle - he saved himself and his family. Unfortunately, he and his parents were the only ones who succeeded. The rest of the family was murdered. One uncle, hidden by Christians throughout the war, was killed during the Kielce Pogrom in 1946. Stanisław Lem spoke of these things incredibly rarely, and he never wrote. I know that from family connections. After the war, the family settled in Krakow, although Lviv remained the love of his life. He graduated from medical school, but in order to avoid military service, which was then forced for doctors, he failed the final exam, and as a writer he was able to function, he could avoid downloading. After his initial failure, he worked out what he said, the unique style and drama of writing, which went far beyond science fiction. It was rather a philosophical reflection, reflection on the philosophy of culture and technology. He dealt with the consequences of misuse of technology by the people who created it, anticipating ethical problems related to the development of artificial intelligence, warning against unintelligible anthropocentrism, investigating the role of the case as a cause force. He's ahead of that by generations. He is an outstanding writer and philosopher, an outstanding Pole, he deserves it. The Polish Coalition Club votes in favour. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u503	High Izbo! In 2001, at the initiative of Michael Kawecki, Cyprian Kamil Norwid symbolically returned to his homeland, which he had to leave as only 20 years old. The urn with the earth extracted from his grave at Montmorency cemetery near Paris stood in the crypt of Wawel. When making a film, Norwid's return, I visited many places connected with the poet, among others. Kazimierz's factory in Ivra, where Norwid, after years of suffering, was blind and deaf, died. Unfortunately, we will never get to know the last of his works, because Polish nuns who manage the shelter burned the manuscripts of a freakish old man. Lies! Lie! The most important thing, however, is that what remains of it is considered to be great, and it itself - an outstanding Polish poet, the last of the four great romantics, the first positivist whom the Young Poland discovered and recognized as its forerunner. Norwid lived outside Poland in Dresden, Berlin, Venice, Florence, where he studied sculpture, Rome, Paris, London and several months in New York. The poet-troller was undoubtedly a European and a citizen of the world. He fought with a pen, for example. to abolish slavery in the United States. But he always missed Poland, which he could not return to. The unfulfilled love of his life was the pianist Maria Kalergis, who rejected the youthful feelings of a twenty - three - year - old. He never started a family. The artist was also a cartoonist and painter. Only four of his oil paintings survived to this day. Sometimes he had sponsors, he worked in a graphics studio, but he also used to chop wood. But he spent most of his life in poverty, loneliness and oblivion. Unlike other romantics, he rarely addressed topics related to the history of Poland, and if any, he included them in a wider context and in general human categories. His work is a new type of romantic poetry, because the fourth word was not focused on the nation and glorifying Polish martyrdom. On the contrary, he argued with the ubiquitous cult of heroism, lack of a sense of civic duty, Polish backwardness, primitiveism and mediocrity. He focused on man and his duties and daily matters. Not on heightened feelings, but on thought and work. He thought poetry should force intellectual effort. His contemporaries accused him of darkness, strange and incomprehensible style, understatements, and weirdness. He was a deeply believing Christian, a thinker, but not a devout. In his day he was an alien, today he inspires other artists: from Niemen to Budka Suffler to Tinyczuk. At the creators of disco polo vain (the bell) look for Norwid inspiration. I don't think he could count on a grant from Gliński. Mrs Bożena Żrołowska, club of the Polish People's Party. Well, maybe I can finish without the microphone. ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u517	Dear Marshal! The time of war is a time which defines their present reality, their everyday life from the sign of disaster and the apocalypse fulfilled. War reality did not allow them to enjoy youth, happiness, love, or other values. Instead of allegiance, they chose an uneven fight against the occupier. The Columbus were poets of action, they acted and proved the truth of their words in life. They saw the world of destruction, death, the fall of the old world, the breakdown of moral values, and so they were called the generation of the apocalypse fulfilled. The war determined their youth, took away their maturity, brought death. Most of them died during the Warsaw Uprising. The poet grew in the patriotic spirit of the performances of his father fighting in legions and grandfather, the January insurgent. Baczyński attended the Junior High School. Stefan Batory and wrote poems before he left this school. He was endowed with extraordinary art skills and planned to study at the Academy of Fine Arts in Warsaw to become an illustrator and graphic artist. He also thought about studying in France. The outbreak of World War II and the family tragedy, that is, the death of Father Krzysztof in July 1939, destroyed these plans forever. Like many of his friends, Baczyński was active in scouting and was a member of the sports club. During the war he studied Polish literature at the underground university. He was also associated with the Socialist Flame Group and the Road publishing house. However, the left-wing sympathies of the young poet significantly weakened after 17 September 1939, and eventually cooled down after the discovery of graves in Katyn in April 1943. Baczyński was heartbroken to active participation in armed operations, although the commanders were reluctant to risk the life of a well-known and valued poet, whom he was already in that period. He was involved in conspiracy activities and served in the battalion of Zośka and then Parasol. The poet in his timeless poems raises such topics as fidelity, love, and homeland. Baczyński is not afraid to show human fear of death, tragic choice and sacrifice his life for freedom. [[♪ The bell ♪]] Just a moment, if I may, Marshal. The tragic death of a 23-year-old poet did not destroy his literary work. Baczyński reached maturity in such an amazingly fast way that his work is a sentence fully spoken and finished, it is a poetry of high and independent flight, although this flight lasted short. But to us who read his poetry will always be accompanied by this persistent yearning for all those poems he has not written. On behalf of the Polish Coalition Club, I recommend this resolution to the High House. Thank you.

ParlaMint-PL_2020-11-27-sejm-21-3.u537	Thank you very much. Mrs. Marshal! Ladies and gentlemen, ladies and gentlemen, Tadeusz Różewicz, whom we are discussing today, is a Wrocławian for nearly half his life. But above all, he was a poet who was painfully experienced by the Second World War, which he had partially spent in guerrilla forest troops, fought with a gun in his hand, closely watching suffering and death. And it was in such circumstances that he began his literary work seriously, on which the cruelty of war and the nonsense of killing made such a strong impression. Fortunately, this experience did not direct him towards passiveness like several other Polish artists, but led him to search for new forms of expression, creation of a new language of poetry and drama. In this way he contributed to the renewal of post-war Polish literature. Tadeusz Różewicz is not only a forerunner, an innovative creator, but also a brave poet. In his work he challenged the novels of the Polish People's Republic and openly mocked the official language, at that time full of communes, empty-headedness and mindless shampoo. This inspiring author introduced many avant-garde writing techniques into Polish literature and originally engaged in discussions with important currents in world literature, such as the theater of absurdity. No wonder his work was translated into 49 languages, and he himself became one of the most popular Polish authors in the world. No doubt he deserved the Nobel Prize for Literature. The Master was very pleased with all foreign editions of his works. Not because he was vain, but because he very much believed in transnational brotherhood, whose foundation he saw, among others. in reconciliation of old mortal enemies, including the Polish-German reconciliation. The physical manifestation of these beliefs was his sincere long-standing friendship with Günter Grass. He was very pleased with the construction of the European Community, but shortly before his death he felt what we are unfortunately witnessing today. For he predicted a serious crisis of values, the fall of authority and chaos in the world order we know. Looking at what is happening today both in international politics and in our Polish backyard, one can sadly say that master intuition and this time, unfortunately, has not failed. Let me finally, as a Wrocławian, recall the anecdote heard from Rafał Dutkiewicz, my friend and friend of the late poet. In one of the talks with the then president of Wrocław, Tadeusz Różewicz strongly opposed the construction of the posthumous monument, and especially if it were to take the form of a very popular so-called today. A bench where you can sit down with the person you're dedicated to. He argued: in my life, I choose my own company, [[♪ The bell ♪]] And on such a bench - will I have an influence on who will sit next to me? Such, ironic, infernally brilliant and with a sense of humor, was this extraordinary creator. Club Coalition Poland - Polish People's Party, Union of European Democrats, Conservatives supports the resolution establishing 2021 Year of Tadeusz Różewicz and expresses great satisfaction that this is how the 100th anniversary of his birthday will be honored. Thank you. [[Applause]]

On the night of 29 to 30 November 1830 members of the conspiracy of lieutenant Piotr Wysocki, established at the Podchorażych Piechota School in Warsaw, attacked Belvedere and then won Arsenal, supported by troops of the Polish army and the inhabitants of Warsaw. As a result of the fights and subsequent negotiations by the Interim Government, Russian troops were withdrawn from the capital with the hated Grand Duke Constantine. Warsaw was free. This is how the November Uprising began. The Sejm of the Republic of Poland pays tribute to Polish soldiers who fought heroically against the Russian aggressor in battles near Stock, Wawr, Olszynka Grochowska, Białołęka, Dęb Wielki, Igania and Ostrołęka. We keep in mind generals and officers, leaders of the November Uprising, including Józef Chłopicki, Ignacy Prądzyński, Jan Skrzynecki, Józef Dwernicki, Józef Bem and Józef Sowiński. The Sejm of the Republic of Poland honors Members, Senators, members of the National Government, participants of guerrilla fights in the Kingdom of Poland and Lithuania and all those who, during the November Uprising, often dedicated their lives, served the Polish cause. On the 190th anniversary of the outbreak of the November Uprising, the largest military uprising in the period of partitions, the Polish Parliament expresses deep respect for its participants - our ancestors, great patriots seeking Poland's independence. I propose that we adopt this resolution by acclamation. [[Applause]] Thank you very much.
